# Turn Analysis -> Response Planner -> Skeleton Builder Batch

- Generation mode: BasePlusVariants
- Builder version: SkeletonBuilder.PostArchitectureRevamp_v2
- Source cases: 300
- Response evaluations: 1800

Overlap and gap-fill ratios are measured as shares of selected skeleton hits. Ending occupancy is selected ending hits divided by selected hits. Segment difference is the L1 distance between source and selected segment hit distributions.
